Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the ceramics framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 411. The resulting operator achieves bound O(637/n^1).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the ceramics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^411 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 637-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
